How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Golden Carrot
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Brewing ingredients
Golden Carrot is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Golden Carrot.
[Golden Carrot]
Also known as: Gold Carrot
Type: Brewing Ingredient
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]); Medium Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]); Large Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]])
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Alchemist]]
Buy: 15 [[Coins]]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Carrot]] 8x [[Gold Nugget]]
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 7.333 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOLDEN_CARROT
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; COMMON
Golden Carrot is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item. It can be bought from the [[Alchemist]] for 15 [[Coins]]. It requires no [[Collections|collection]] to unlock crafting.
## Obtaining
Golden Carrot can be bought from the [[Alchemist]] for 15 [[Coins]] each.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Carrot]] 8x [[Gold Nugget]]|Recipe: [[Gold Nugget]] ×8 + [[Carrot]] → Golden Carrot|
## Usage
### [[Night Vision Potion|Night Vision I Potion]]
Recipe: Golden Carrot → [[Night Vision Potion]], [[Night Vision Potion]], [[Night Vision Potion]]
### Garden Visitors
When visiting [[The Garden]], the [[Gold Forger]] requests [[Golden Carrot]] 1,024 (16 stacks) Golden Carrots.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Golden Carrot.|
|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Removed 640 daily buy limit.|
|Unknown Date|Decreased NPC buy price from 8 [[Coins]] to 7 [[Coins]].|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased NPC buy price from 7 [[Coins]] to 15 [[Coins]].|

Item ID: GRASS

Grass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block.
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Grass can be found on the player's starting [[Private Island]] and plots in [[The Garden]]. It can also be spawned from the [[Roofed Forest Island]].
### Starter Chest
7 Grass can be found in the starting [[Chest]] that spawns on a new [[Private Island]].
### Trades
4 Grass can be traded in exchange for 4 [[Dirt]] in the [[Trades]] menu.
### Shop Purchase
Grass can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 2 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Grass can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].

# Gravel
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Blocks, Pages with UIs, More info needed, Crafting ingredients, Gravel Collection, Collections, Items
Gravel is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Gravel.
[Gravel]
Type: Collection block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Tool ([[Tools]]): Shovel ([[Shovels]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Pat]]
Buy: 4.33 [[Coins]]
Sell: 3 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRAVEL
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Gravel is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] collection block found in a small ravine in the [[Spider's Den]]. It can also be purchased from [[Pat]].
## Collection
Collecting gravel increases the player's gravel [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of gravel.
Note: Although there is a chance for flint when mining gravel, it will always count towards your collection regardless of what you receive.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Gravel Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Flint Shovel]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|Portal to the Spider's Den ([[Portals]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Sharpness]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Enchanted Flint]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[First Strike]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Critical Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|15,000|[[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|+5,000 [[Mining]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Gravel generates near the ravine in [[Spider's Den]], located at [[Coordinates|-257, 70, -327]]. Commonly spawns within the lower section of the Den.
#### Ticks Thresholds
Show/Hide Tick Thresholds
Mining Time (in Ticks): Necessary and sufficient conditions while mining…
Mining Time (in Ticks): [[Gravel]] Gravel
15: Haste I ([[Haste Potion]])
14: [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
13: Haste II ([[Haste Potion]])
12: Haste III ([[Haste Potion]])
11: [More Info Needed]
10: Haste IV ([[Haste Potion]])
9: [[Wooden Shovel]]
8: [[Wooden Shovel]]; Haste I ([[Haste Potion]])
7: [[Wooden Shovel]]; Haste II ([[Haste Potion]])
6: [[Wooden Shovel]]; Haste III ([[Haste Potion]])
5: [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste IV ([[Haste Potion]]); 1 M. Penalty
4 (Softcap): Efficiency I ([[Efficiency]]) [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste IV ([[Haste Potion]]); 1 M. Penalty
3: [[Iron Shovel]]
2: [[Golden Shovel]]
1 (Instamine): Efficiency III ([[Efficiency]]) [[Golden Shovel]]
1 (Instamine): [[Golden Shovel]]; Haste III ([[Haste Potion]])
### Merchants
Gravel can be purchased from [[Pat]] for 4.33 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to the Spider's Den ([[Portals]])|Gravel III (3)|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Web]] [[Gravel]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Gravel|Recipe: [[Web]] x16 ×3 + Gravel x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Gravel Minion I ([[Gravel Minion]])|Gravel I (1)|1x [[Wooden Shovel]] [[Gravel]] 80x Gravel|Recipe: Gravel x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Shovel]] → [[Gravel Minion]]|
|Gravel Minion II ([[Gravel Minion]])|Gravel I (1)|1x Gravel Minion I ([[Gravel Minion]]) [[Gravel]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Gravel|Recipe: Gravel x20 ×8 + [[Gravel Minion]] → [[Gravel Minion]]|
|Gravel Minion III ([[Gravel Minion]])|Gravel I (1)|1x Gravel Minion II ([[Gravel Minion]]) [[Gravel]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Gravel|Recipe: Gravel x40 ×8 + [[Gravel Minion]] → [[Gravel Minion]]|
|Gravel Minion IV ([[Gravel Minion]])|Gravel I (1)|1x Gravel Minion III ([[Gravel Minion]]) [[Gravel]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Gravel|Recipe: Gravel x64 ×8 + [[Gravel Minion]] → [[Gravel Minion]]|
|[[Coarse Dirt]]|None|2x [[Dirt]] [[Gravel]] 2x Gravel|Recipe: [[Dirt]] ×2 + Gravel ×2 → [[Coarse Dirt]] x4|

|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Gravel.|
|Aug 15, 2019|[[Flint]] now count towards Gravel collection.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Gravel now respawns faster in the [[Spider's Den]].|

Gunpowder can be obtained from [[Creeper Minion|Creeper Minions]].
### Shop Purchase
Gunpowder can be purchased from the [[Combat Merchant]] in the [[Hub Island]] for 10 [[Coins]] each.
## Usage
### Brewing
Gunpowder can be used when [[Potions|Brewing]] to create splash [[Potions]] but at half the duration.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Creeper Hat]]|Gunpowder II (2)|[[Gunpowder]] 8x Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder ×8 → [[Creeper Hat]]|
|Creeper Minion I ([[Creeper Minion]])|Gunpowder I (1)|1x [[Wooden Sword]] [[Gunpowder]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Sword]] → [[Creeper Minion]]|
|Creeper Minion II ([[Creeper Minion]])|Gunpowder I (1)|1x Creeper Minion I ([[Creeper Minion]]) [[Gunpowder]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder x20 ×8 + [[Creeper Minion]] → [[Creeper Minion]]|
|Creeper Minion III ([[Creeper Minion]])|Gunpowder I (1)|1x Creeper Minion II ([[Creeper Minion]]) [[Gunpowder]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder x40 ×8 + [[Creeper Minion]] → [[Creeper Minion]]|
|Creeper Minion IV ([[Creeper Minion]])|Gunpowder I (1)|1x Creeper Minion III ([[Creeper Minion]]) [[Gunpowder]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder x64 ×8 + [[Creeper Minion]] → [[Creeper Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Gunpowder IV (4)|[[Gunpowder]] 160x Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|
|[[Firework Rocket]]|None|1x [[Paper]] [[Gunpowder]] 1x Gunpowder|Recipe: [[Paper]] + Gunpowder → [[Firework Rocket]]|
|[[Firework Rocket]]|None|1x [[Paper]] 1x [[Firework Star]] [[Gunpowder]] 1x Gunpowder|Recipe: [[Firework Star]] + [[Paper]] + Gunpowder → [[Firework Rocket]]|
|[[TNT]]|None|4x [[Sand]] [[Gunpowder]] 5x Gunpowder|Recipe: Gunpowder ×5 + [[Sand]] ×4 → [[TNT]]|
|[[Firework Star]]|None|[[Gunpowder]] 1x Gunpowder 1x Dye ([[Vanilla Objects/Dyes]])|Recipe: Gunpowder + [[Bone Meal]]/[[Orange Dye]]/[[Magenta Dye]]/[[Light Blue Dye]]/[[Dandelion Yellow]]/[[Lime Dye]]/[[Pink Dye]]/[[Gray Dye]]/[[Light Gray Dye]]/[[Cyan Dye]]/[[Purple Dye]]/[[Lapis Lazuli]]/[[Cocoa Beans]]/[[Cactus Green]]/[[Rose Red]]/[[Ink Sac]] → [[Firework Star]]|
## Tips
- While not a cheap, or easy to obtain weapon, the [[Flower of Truth]] is a very viable weapon for farming [[Sneaky Creeper|Sneaky Creepers]], when the player first starts the Gunpowder collection. A cheaper way is to use an Artisanal Shortbow. The bow doesn't need any special enchants.
  - This is because the only way to farm them, without a minion, is in the [[Gunpowder Mines]], where they are invisible until they are next to the player.
  - The [[Flower of Truth]] seeks onto them, even when the player cannot see them, killing them and ricocheting to other creepers.
- One way of getting gunpowder is following someone else around the Gunpowder Mines and killing creepers that spawn behind them.
## Trivia
- The internal item ID of this item is SULPHUR. There is an actual item called [[Sulphur]] with the ID SULPHUR_ORE.
- For a few weeks after the release of the Crimson Isle Update, the tooltip for Gunpowder had the quote "Give me some Sulphur and I'll make a wand. Give me all the Sulphur and I'll conquer the world." - Unnamed Mage. The message was later moved to the tooltip for [[Sulphur]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Gunpowder.|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Fixed an issue with brewing where adding Gunpowder followed by [[Redstone Dust]] wouldn’t halve the duration.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Fixed a [[Crimson Isle]] daily Faction Quest ([[Faction Quests]]) that asks for Gunpowder instead of [[Sulphur]]|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Fixed the clickable Gunpowder collection level-up message opening up the [[Sulphur]] Collection.|
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Gunpowder to the [[Creeperlobber]] and Goblin Creeper ([[Creeper]]) loot tables.|

### Private Island

# Hoes
Aliases: Hoe
Categories: Vanilla objects, Hoes
Hoes is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Hoes.
Hoes are used in [[Farming]] to till [[Dirt]] into [[Farmland]] for planting some [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crops]]. They do not grant any [[Farming Fortune]] or [[Crop Fortune]] and are not meant for harvesting crops.

|[[Hoe of Greater Tilling]]|[[Hoe of Greater Tilling]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Tills a 5x5 area of farmland at a time|[[Anita]] ( 1 Bronze Medal ([[Medals]]), 5 [[Jacob's Ticket]])|
|[[Hoe of Greatest Tilling]]|[[Hoe of Greatest Tilling]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Tills a full row of farmland at a time|[[Anita]] ( 2 Silver Medal ([[Medals]]), 5 [[Jacob's Ticket]])|
|[[Hoe of No Tilling]]|[[Hoe of No Tilling]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Can till and untill farmland|[[Shen's Auction]]|

Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); [[Winter Sack]]
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Builder]] [[Sherry]]
Daily Limit (A limit on how much items of this type a player can buy in one day.): 640
Buy: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
Sell: 0.5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ICE
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Ice is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block in the [[Mining]] collection. It can be obtained in a number of ways, such as being bought from the [[Builder]] Merchant, or from [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Collection
Collecting ice increases the player's ice [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of ice.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Ice Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Ice Bait]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Packed Ice]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|500|[[Enchanted Ice]] Recipe|+4|
|V|1,000|[[Magical Water Bucket]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Glacial Ring]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Enchanted Packed Ice]] Recipe; [[Ice Cube]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|50,000|[[Glacial Artifact]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|100,000|[[Frozen Blaze Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|X|250,000|[[Frozen Scythe]] Recipe|+4|
|XI|500,000|[[Glacial Scythe]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Ice can be mined with a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]], [[Drills|Drill]], or [[Axes|Axe]] enchanted with [[Silk Touch|Silk Touch I]]. Mining Ice grants +0.5 [[Mining]] experience.
Ice cannot be mined on any of the public islands, except for [[Jerry's Workshop]], found at the [[Jerry Pond]], [[Sunken Jerry Pond]], [[Reflective Pond]], or [[Glacial Cave]]. Within the [[Glacial Cave]], there is [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]], which can grant other forms of ice.
On a [[Private Island]], players can use the [[Biome Stick|Taiga Biome Stick]] to create a Taiga biome and wait for the water to turn into ice, although this is quite slow.

### Minions
Ice can be obtained from [[Ice Minion|Ice Minions]].
### Fishing
Fishing with the [[Icy Sinker]] applied grants [[Ice]] 1 Ice when catching a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] or item.

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ice Minion I ([[Ice Minion]])|Ice I (1)|1x [[Wooden Pickaxe]] [[Ice]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Ice|Recipe: Ice x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Pickaxe]] → [[Ice Minion]]|
|Ice Minion II ([[Ice Minion]])|Ice I (1)|1x Ice Minion I ([[Ice Minion]]) [[Ice]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Ice|Recipe: Ice x20 ×8 + [[Ice Minion]] → [[Ice Minion]]|
|Ice Minion III ([[Ice Minion]])|Ice I (1)|1x Ice Minion II ([[Ice Minion]]) [[Ice]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Ice|Recipe: Ice x40 ×8 + [[Ice Minion]] → [[Ice Minion]]|
|Ice Minion IV ([[Ice Minion]])|Ice I (1)|1x Ice Minion III ([[Ice Minion]]) [[Ice]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Ice|Recipe: Ice x64 ×8 + [[Ice Minion]] → [[Ice Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Ice]]|Ice IV (4)|[[Ice]] 160x Ice|Recipe: Ice x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Ice]]|
|[[Ice Bait]]|Ice II (2)|[[Ice]] 1x Ice 1x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: Ice + [[Raw Cod]] → [[Ice Bait]]|
|[[Packed Ice]]|None|[[Ice]] 9x Ice|Recipe: Ice ×9 → [[Packed Ice]]|
## Trivia
- [[Frost Walker]]-enchanted boots used to count towards the ice collection, but this was removed after an unknown update.
  - While ice generated from [[Frost Walker]] does not count towards the collection and does not grant [[Mining]] experience, it does count towards and can activate the [[Compact]] enchantment to produce [[Enchanted Ice]].
- The player can purchase 40 [[Enchanted Ice]] from the [[Builder]] each day.
- The ice required to make all special crafts amounts to 26,786,897. That's the total for all 12 tiers of the [[Ice Minion]], [[Ice Bait]], [[Magical Water Bucket]], [[Glacial Scythe]], [[Frozen Blaze Armor]], [[Auger Rod]], [[Yeti Sword]], and [[Glacial Artifact]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Ice.|
|Unknown Date|Ice created from [[Frost Walker]] no longer contribute to the player's Ice Collection.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Added [[Ice Cube]] to the Ice Collection.|

# Invisibility Potion
Categories: Vanilla objects, Items, Potions
Invisibility Potion is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Invisibility.
[Invisibility Potion]
Type: Potion ([[Potions]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POTION
Potion ID: INVISIBILITY
Tooltip: Invisibility I (3:00); Grants invisibility from players and mobs.; COMMON
The Invisibility Potion is a BUFF [[Potions|Potion]] that temporarily makes the user invisible.
## Stats
Potion:
Invisibility I Potion: Invisibility Ⅰ
3:00: Grants invisibility from players and mobs.
[Brewing]
## Obtaining
### Brewing
|Tier|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Brewing Stand]]) Layout|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|[[Night Vision Potion]] [[Fermented Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Fermented Spider Eye]] → [[Night Vision Potion]], [[Night Vision Potion]], [[Night Vision Potion]]|
#### Modifying
Certain brewing ingredients can be added during the brewing process to modify the potion. Other mechanics can also extend the length of the potion.
Modifiers:
|Ingredient|Effect|
|-|-|
|[[Redstone Dust]]|+8:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|+16:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|+16:00|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]]|+16:00|
|[[Gunpowder]]|Duration ÷ 2; Splash|
|[[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Splash|
|[[Potion Affinity Talisman]]|+10% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Ring]]|+25% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Artifact]]|+50% Duration|
|[[Parrot Pet]]|+40% Duration|
|[[Alchemy]]|+𝑥 Duration|
## Trivia
- While monsters will have a much harder time noticing a player under the effects of Invisibility, they will still attack like normal once the player's presence is known.
- Equipping [[Sorrow Armor]] overrides the timer for the Invisibility effect, as un-equipping it clears the player of that effect regardless of another source.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Invisibility Potion.|

### Private Island

Merchants: [[Mining Merchant]] [[Iron Forger]]
Buy: 5.5 [[Coins]]; 5 [[Coins]]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: IRON_INGOT
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Iron Ingot is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] collection item.
## Collection
Collecting iron ingots increases the player's iron ingot [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of iron ingots.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Iron Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Golem Hat]] Recipe; [[Prospecting Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Protection]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Budget Hopper]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Golem Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Enchanted Iron Block]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Golem Sword]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|[[Enchanted Hopper]] Recipe; [[Personal Deletor 4000]] Recipe|+4|
|X|100,000|[[Personal Deletor 5000]] Recipe|+4|
|XI|200,000|[[Personal Deletor 6000]] Recipe|+4|
|XII|400,000|[[Personal Deletor 7000]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Natural generation
Iron Ingot can be found naturally in the form of [[Ores|Iron Ore]] in [[Gold Mine]], [[Gunpowder Mines]], [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
### Smelting
|Ingredients|Smelting recipe|
|-|-|
|1 Iron Ore ([[Ores]])|Recipe: [[Ores]] + [[Coal]] → Iron Ingot|
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Block of Iron]]|Recipe: [[Block of Iron]] → Iron Ingot x9|
### Purchase
Prices displayed by the merchant aren't always the same as the amount it charges for an item. This table lists the actual prices, not the ones displayed.

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mining Merchant]]|5.5|22|27.5|55||176|352|
|[[Iron Forger]]|5||25|50|100|160|320|

# Iron Pickaxe
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Tools, Mining
Iron Pickaxe is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Iron Pickaxe.
[Iron Pickaxe]
Type: Pickaxe ([[Pickaxes]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: 25; [[Breaking Power]]: 3; [[Mining Speed]]: 160
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: IRON_PICKAXE
Tooltip: Breaking Power 3; Damage: +25; Mining Speed: +160; This item can be reforged!; COMMON PICKAXE
The Iron Pickaxe is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] that can be crafted.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Stick]] 3x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Iron Pickaxe|
### Quest
Upon speaking to the [[Lazy Miner]] in the [[Gold Mine]], they task the player with finding their lost pickaxe, located at [[Coordinates|-18, 24, -305]]. This pickaxe comes with [[Efficiency|Efficiency I]] and [[Smelting Touch|Smelting Touch I]].
### Shop Purchase
An Iron Pickaxe with [[Smelting Touch|Smelting Touch I]] can be purchased from the [[Iron Forger]] for 60 [[Coins]].
After obtaining the [[Lazy Miner]]'s pickaxe once, more of them can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 200 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Iron Pickaxe [[Damage]]: +25 [[Breaking Power]]: +3 [[Mining Speed]]: +160
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Iron Pickaxe.|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added +160 [[Mining Speed]] and +3 [[Breaking Power]] to the Iron Pickaxe.|

|VI|2,000|+2,000 [[Foraging]]Experience|+4|
|VII|5,000|[[Treecapitator]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|10,000|+10,000 [[Foraging]]Experience|+4|
|IX|25,000|+10,000 [[Foraging]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
### Foraging
Jungle Log can be found on the [[Jungle Island]], after reaching [[Foraging|Foraging V (5)]]. Breaking [[Jungle Log]] Jungle Logs grants +6 [[Foraging]] XP.
### Minions
Jungle Logs can be obtained from [[Jungle Minion|Jungle Minions]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv90]|[[Soul of the Alpha]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Merchants
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 1x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 5x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 10x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 32x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lumber Merchant]]|5 [[Coins]]​|25 [[Coins]]​|50 [[Coins]]​|160 [[Coins]]​|320 [[Coins]]|
## Usage
Jungle Log is used for some basic crafting, such as for crafting [[Jungle Minion|Jungle Minions]] and [[Portals|Portal to Jungle Island]].
It can also be used as fuel in a [[Furnace]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to Jungle Island ([[Portals]])|Jungle Log III (3)|16x [[Ender Pearl]] [[Jungle Log]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x16 ×8 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|[[Enchanted Charcoal]]|Coal V (5) ([[Coal]])|32 Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) (Vanilla) 128 (2 stacks) [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] x32 ×6 + [[Oak Log]] x32/[[Birch Log]] x32/[[Spruce Log]] x32/[[Dark Oak Log]] x32/[[Acacia Log]] x32/Jungle Log x32 → [[Enchanted Charcoal]]|
|[[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|Jungle Log III (3)|[[Jungle Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|
|Jungle Minion I ([[Jungle Minion]])|Jungle Log I (1)|1x [[Wooden Axe]] [[Jungle Log]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Axe]] → [[Jungle Minion]]|
|Jungle Minion II ([[Jungle Minion]])|Jungle Log I (1)|1x Jungle Minion I ([[Jungle Minion]]) [[Jungle Log]] 160x Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x20 ×8 + [[Jungle Minion]] → [[Jungle Minion]]|
|Jungle Minion III ([[Jungle Minion]])|Jungle Log I (1)|1x Jungle Minion II ([[Jungle Minion]]) [[Jungle Log]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x40 ×8 + [[Jungle Minion]] → [[Jungle Minion]]|
|Jungle Minion IV ([[Jungle Minion]])|Jungle Log I (1)|1x Jungle Minion III ([[Jungle Minion]]) [[Jungle Log]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x64 ×8 + [[Jungle Minion]] → [[Jungle Minion]]|
|Jungle Planks ([[Planks]])|None|[[Jungle Log]] 1x Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log → [[Planks]] x4|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Ocelot Pet]]|Jungle Log III (3)|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] [[Jungle Log]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Jungle Log|Recipe: Jungle Log x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [[Ocelot Pet]]|

Lapis Lazuli (usually known as Lapis) is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla item obtained via [[Mining]] with at least 2 [[Breaking Power]]. It can be collected in [[Lapis Quarry]], [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]] by mining [[Ores|Lapis Lazuli Ore]]. The main use for mining Lapis Lazuli is getting XP levels, as it drops many experience orbs.
## Collection
Collecting lapis lazuli increases the player's lapis lazuli [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of lapis lazuli.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|250|[[Lapis Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|500|[[Experience Bottle]] Recipe|+4|
|III|1,000|[[Lapis Pickaxe]] Recipe; Experience ([[Experience (Enchantment)]]) Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|IV|2,000|[[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] Recipe|+4|
|V|10,000|[[Grand Experience Bottle]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|25,000|[[Experience Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|50,000|[[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|100,000|[[Titanic Experience Bottle]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|150,000|[[Experience Artifact]] Recipe|+4|
|X|250,000|[[Textbook]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Lapis Lazuli is obtained by mining the [[Ores|Lapis Lazuli Ores]] found in [[Lapis Quarry]], [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]]. Mining [[Ores|Lapis Lazuli Ore]] grants +7 [[Mining|Mining XP]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] → Lapis Lazuli x9|
### [[Stranded]] only (This content is only available on the Stranded game mode.)
Lapis Lazuli can be obtained from [[Lapis Zombie]], which can be spawned by placing a [[Sea Lantern Hat]] in [[Zombie Minion]]'s upgrade slot.

### Crafting

|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Experience I Potion ([[Experience Potion]])|Lapis Lazuli VI (6)|[[Lapis Lazuli]] 1x Lapis Lazuli 3x [[Awkward Potion]]|Recipe: Lapis Lazuli → [[Experience Potion]], [[Experience Potion]], [[Experience Potion]]|
## Bugs
- The [[Mining|Spelunker]] perk from the [[Mining]] Skill does not apply to Lapis Lazuli, regardless of how it was mined or where it was found. The reason is currently unknown, but may be related to the fact that [[Ores|Lapis Ore]] drops multiple items by default.
- The [[Andesite Whetstone|Refined]] reforge on pickaxe give the player [[Enchanted Ink Sac]] instead of [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]. It is linked to the fact that the Lapis Lazuli is a Dye with a damage value of 4 and the Ink Sac a Dye with a damage value of 0. Receiving an Enchanted Ink Sac this way increase the [[Ink Sac|Ink Sac Collection]].
## Trivia
- Lapis Lazuli was one of the first collections to have a level 10 crafting recipe.

# Lava Bucket
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items
Lava Bucket is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Lava Bucket.
[Lava Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Sell: 8 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LAVA_BUCKET
Tooltip: COMMON
A Lava Bucket is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be used to create [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].
## Obtaining
### Trades
A Lava Bucket can be obtained in exchange for 20 [[Coins]] in the [[Trades]] menu after unlocking [[Magma Cream|Magma Cream V (5)]].
### Shop Purchase
A Lava Bucket can be purchased for 20 [[Coins]] from the [[Builder]].
### Buckets
A Lava Bucket can be obtained by filling up a [[Bucket]] from a lava source.
### Starting Chest
A Lava Bucket can be found in the starting [[Chest]] that appears on a new [[Private Island]].
## Usage
### Lava
An Lava Bucket can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]] to create a [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] source block. Doing this will turn the Lava Bucket back into a [[Bucket]].
## Tips
- The [[Magical Lava Bucket]] is a version of the Lava Bucket that does not turn into a [[Bucket]] when used.

# Leaves
Aliases: Acacia Leaves, Birch Leaves, Dark Oak Leaves, Jungle Leaves, Oak Leaves, Spruce Leaves
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Blocks, Crafting ingredients, Oak Log Collection, Spruce Log Collection, Birch Log Collection, Dark Oak Log Collection, Acacia Log Collection, Jungle Log Collection
Leaves is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Leaves.
[Leaves]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Location: [[Forest]] [[The Park]]
Tool ([[Tools]]): [[Shears]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Builder]]
Buy: 4 [[Coins]]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LEAVES (Oak Leaves); LEAVES:1 (Spruce Leaves); LEAVES:2 (Birch Leaves); LEAVES:3 (Jungle Leaves); LEAVES_2 (Acacia Leaves); LEAVES_2:1 (Dark Oak Leaves)
Tooltip: COMMON
There are 6 types of Leaves, one for each type of tree in Minecraft.
## List
|Img|Type|Trade Unlock|Location|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Leaves]]|Oak|Oak Log II (2) ([[Oak Log]])|[[Forest]] / [[The Park]]|
|[[Leaves]]|Spruce|Spruce Log II (2) ([[Spruce Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Leaves]]|Birch|Birch Log II (2) ([[Birch Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Leaves]]|Jungle|Jungle Log II (2) ([[Jungle Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Leaves]]|Acacia|Acacia Log II (2) ([[Acacia Log]])|[[The Park]]|
|[[Leaves]]|Dark Oak|Dark Oak Log II (2) ([[Dark Oak Log]])||
## Obtaining
Leaves can be obtained by breaking them in the [[Forest]] or [[The Park]] with [[Shears]] or a tool with the [[Silk Touch]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]].
Leaves can also be bought from the [[Builder]].
#### Trades
|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|Oak Log II (2) ([[Oak Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Birch Log II (2) ([[Birch Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Spruce Log II (2) ([[Spruce Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Dark Oak Log II (2) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Acacia Log II (2) ([[Acacia Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
|Jungle Log II (2) ([[Jungle Log]])|[[Saplings]] →|→ [[Leaves]]|
## Usage

|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|Jungle Log IV (4) ([[Jungle Log]])|[[Leaves]] x5 →|→ [[Vines]]|
### Crafting

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wood Affinity Talisman]]|Oak Log VII (7) ([[Oak Log]])|1x [[Enchanted Oak Log]] 8x Oak Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Oak Log]] → [[Wood Affinity Talisman]]|
|Forest Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Oak Log V (5) ([[Oak Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Oak Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|Birch Forest Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Birch Log VI (6) ([[Birch Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Birch Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|Jungle Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Jungle Log V (5) ([[Jungle Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Jungle Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|Taiga Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Spruce Log V (5) ([[Spruce Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Spruce Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|Savanna Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Acacia Log V (5) ([[Acacia Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Acacia Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|
|Roofed Forest Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Dark Oak Log V (5) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|2x [[Stick]] 64x Dark Oak Leaves ([[Leaves]])|Recipe: [[Leaves]] x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|

# Lever
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items
Lever is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Lever.
[Lever]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LEVER
Tooltip: COMMON
A Lever is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] block that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Stick]] 1x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Stick]] + [[Cobblestone]] → Lever|
### Shop Purchase
A Lever can be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] for 2 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Levers can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. When placed on a [[Private Island]] and activated, it will activate adjacent [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] blocks, but there are [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often this can be done. Redstone is disabled on [[The Garden]].
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|Please note that redstone is currently disabled on this island!|Placing a Lever in [[The Garden]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Lever.|

# Long Grass
Aliases: Short Grass
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs
Long Grass is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Short Grass.
[Long Grass]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LONG_GRASS:1
Tooltip: COMMON
Long Grass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Long Grass can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 4 [[Coins]].
### Trades
One Long Grass can be obtained in exchange for 8 [[Dirt]] in the [[Trades]] menu, after unlocking [[Seeds|Seeds III (3)]].
### Growing
Placed Long Grass can be created by applying [[Bone Meal]] onto [[Grass]] blocks.
## Usage
### Private Island
Long Grass can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. It will not drop itself when broken, even with [[Shears]] or [[Silk Touch]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Long Grass.|

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

|[[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|Magma Cream III (3)|[[Magma Cream]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Magma Cream|Recipe: Magma Cream x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|
|Nether Biome Stick ([[Biome Stick]])|Magma Cream III (3)|2x [[Stick]] [[Magma Cream]] 64x Magma Cream|Recipe: Magma Cream x64 + [[Stick]] ×2 → [[Biome Stick]]|

|Magma Cube Minion II ([[Magma Cube Minion]])|Magma Cream I (1)|1x Magma Cube Minion I ([[Magma Cube Minion]]) [[Magma Cream]] 160x Magma Cream|Recipe: Magma Cream x20 ×8 + [[Magma Cube Minion]] → [[Magma Cube Minion]]|
|Magma Cube Minion III ([[Magma Cube Minion]])|Magma Cream I (1)|1x Magma Cube Minion II ([[Magma Cube Minion]]) [[Magma Cream]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Magma Cream|Recipe: Magma Cream x40 ×8 + [[Magma Cube Minion]] → [[Magma Cube Minion]]|
|Magma Cube Minion IV ([[Magma Cube Minion]])|Magma Cream I (1)|1x Magma Cube Minion III ([[Magma Cube Minion]]) [[Magma Cream]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Magma Cream|Recipe: Magma Cream x64 ×8 + [[Magma Cube Minion]] → [[Magma Cube Minion]]|
### Brewing Ingredient
|Name|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Fire Resistance I Potion ([[Fire Resistance Potion]])|[[Magma Cream]] Magma Cream 1–3x [[Awkward Potion]]|Recipe: Magma Cream → [[Fire Resistance Potion]], [[Fire Resistance Potion]], [[Fire Resistance Potion]]|
### Quiver
Magma Cream can be placed in the [[Quiver]] to be used as projectiles for the [[Magma Bow]].
### Kuudra Follower Boots
[[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Boots]] requires 1,000,000 in Magma Cream Collection and 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] to be given to [[Kuudra Loremaster]] to claim the boots.
## Trivia
- Magma Cream can be stored in 3 different sacks ([[Nether Sack]], [[Lava Fishing Sack]] and [[Combat Sack]]) which is more than any other resource, except for [[Netherrack]], which can also be stored in 3 sacks.

# Magma Cube
Aliases: Giant Magma Cube

[Magma Cube]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 151
Spawn Location: [[Crimson Fields]]
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 400,000; [[Damage]]: 2,000
[Drops]
Drops: 1–3x [[Magma Cream]] 0–1x UNCOMMON Combat Exp Boost ([[Combat Exp Boost]]) 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]] 0–1x COMMON Magma Cube Pet ([[Magma Cube Pet]]) 0–1x RARE Magma Cube Pet ([[Magma Cube Pet]]) 0–1x [[Magma Chunk]] 0–1x Rampart Armor Piece ([[Rampart Armor]]) 0–1x UNCOMMON Magma Cube Pet ([[Magma Cube Pet]])
XP: +226 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 166
[Metadata]
ID: MAGMA_CUBE_75; FIREBALL_MAGMA_CUBE_75
The Magma Cube is a hostile [[Mob]] that spawns on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
### Crimson Isle
[Lv151] Magma Cubes naturally spawn in the [[Crimson Fields]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
### Private Island
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
In [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], [Lv151] Magma Cubes can spawn naturally in [[Biome Stick|Nether Biomes]].

# Mangrove Log
Aliases: Mangrove, Mangrove Tree
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Mangrove Log Collection, Collections
Mangrove Log is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Mangrove Log.
[Mangrove Log]
Type: Collection item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Skill ([[Skills]]) XP Given: +20 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]]); Medium Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]]); Large Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 14 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MANGROVE_LOG
Tooltip: Collection Item; Sturdy yet flexible, a dance of survival.; Right-click to view recipes!; COMMON
Mangrove Log is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Foraging]] [[Collections|Collection]] item obtained from mangrove trees in the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|500|[[Travel Scroll to Murkwater Loch]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] Recipe|+4|
|III|2,500|[[Mangrove Locket]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|5,000|Mangrove Vine ([[Moonglade Belt]]) Recipe|+4|
|V|10,000|[[Mangrove Gem]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|25,000|[[Mangcore]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|50,000|[[Seagull Shard]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|100,000|[[Mangrove Grippers]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|150,000|+110,000 [[Foraging]]Experience; +1 Foraging Level Cap|+4|
## Obtaining
### Foraging
Mangrove Logs are obtained by breaking mangrove trees in the [[Moonglade Marsh]], specifically within [[Murkwater Loch]], [[Moonglade's Edge]], [[Westbound Wetlands]], [[North Wetlands]] and [[South Wetlands]]. It has a toughness of 50. It grants base +20 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]].
Cutting a Mangrove tree with the correct style allows the player to cut the tree more efficiently. For a Mangrove tree, the player starts with the branches, continues to the trunk, and finishes with the roots.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv29]|[[Tadgang]]|32-64|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv46]|[[Wetwing]]|16|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv46]|[[Wetwing]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv46]|[[Wetwing]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv25]|[[Bogged]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv25]|[[Bogged]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Mangrove Log]]|Mangrove II (2) ([[Mangrove Log]])|[[Mangrove Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Mangrove Log|Recipe: Mangrove Log x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]]|
|[[Travel Scroll to Murkwater Loch]]|Mangrove Log I (1)|16x [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] [[Mangrove Log]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Mangrove Log|Recipe: Mangrove Log x16 ×8 + [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Travel Scroll to Murkwater Loch]]|
## Jade Dragon Pet
The Jade Scale ability of [[Jade Dragon Pet]] provides 15 [[Foraging Fortune]] and 4 [[Sweep]] per every digit in the Mangrove Log collection, up to 120 [[Foraging Fortune]] and 32 [[Sweep]] as 10,000,000 is limit to the ability.
## Mangrove Collector Attribute
The [[Seagull Shard]] grants 1-10 [[Mangrove Fortune]] per every digit in the Mangrove Log collection, up to 8-80 [[Mangrove Fortune]] as 10,000,000 is limit to the ability.

- Mangrove Log without an item model changing its texture

# Map (Item)
Categories: Vanilla objects, Legacy items, Common items, Items, Craftable items
Map (Item) is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Map (Item).
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
This is an article about a Common unobtainable item, for other uses see [[Map (disambiguation)]]
[Map]
Also known as: Filled Map; Empty Map
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 25 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 9x [[Sugar Cane]] 4x [[Iron Ingot]] 1x Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 64 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EMPTY: EMPTY_MAP; FILLED: N/A
Tooltip: COMMON
The Map is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item. It currently has no use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
A map was previously able to be crafted with 8 [[Paper]] and 1 [[Compass]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Compass]] 8x [[Paper]]|Recipe: [[Paper]] ×8 + [[Compass]] → [[Map (disambiguation)]]|
## Trivia
- Maps can be turned into filled maps, however they will not display anything. They also cannot be sold to [[Shops|NPC Shops]].
- There is also a [[Null]] variation of the Map. This is obtained by having two maps in your held slot, and then right-clicking twice, updating the maps into glitched filled maps. After dropping both maps on the ground, one will be a normal map, and the second should be null.
  - Null maps can have damage values from -128 to 128, excluding 0, which appears as a normal map.

# Melon
Aliases: Melon (Block), Melon (block), Melon Block
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Blocks, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Melon Slice Collection
Melon is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Melon.
[Melon]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Location: [[The Barn]]

[Block Drops]

[[Silk Touch]] Drop: Melon (itself)

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 18 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MELON_BLOCK
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Melon is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block and item.
## Obtaining
Melons can be found in [[The Barn]]. When mined without Silk Touch, they drop [[Melon Slice|Melon Slices]], which will increase the player's [[Melon Slice]] collection and [[Farming]] XP. Melons can be obtained via crafting, using the [[Silk Touch]] enchantment, or using a [[Compactor]] in a [[Melon Minion]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Melon Slice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Slice]] ×9 → Melon|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Melon Slice]]|Melon Slice IV (4) ([[Melon Slice]])|[[Melon]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Melon|Recipe: Melon x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Melon Slice]] x9|
|Melon Minion III ([[Melon Minion]])|Melon Slice I (1) ([[Melon Slice]])|1x Melon Minion II ([[Melon Minion]]) [[Melon]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Melon|Recipe: Melon x16 ×8 + [[Melon Minion]] → [[Melon Minion]]|
|Melon Minion IV ([[Melon Minion]])|Melon Slice I (1) ([[Melon Slice]])|1x Melon Minion III ([[Melon Minion]]) [[Melon]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Melon|Recipe: Melon x32 ×8 + [[Melon Minion]] → [[Melon Minion]]|
|Melon Minion V ([[Melon Minion]])|Melon Slice I (1) ([[Melon Slice]])|1x Melon Minion IV ([[Melon Minion]]) [[Melon]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Melon|Recipe: Melon x64 ×8 + [[Melon Minion]] → [[Melon Minion]]|
## Gallery
- Melon in the Greenhouse Melon in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Trivia
- Melons aren't affected by [[Farming Fortune]] or [[Crop Fortune|Melon Slice Fortune]] when mined with a [[Silk Touch]] [[Axes|Axe]]. Instead, it always counts as 9 [[Melon Slice]] collection each, even though the average drop rate without Silk Touch is 5 [[Melon Slice|Melon Slices]].

Milk Bucket is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Milk Bucket.
[Milk Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MILK_BUCKET
Tooltip: COMMON
A Milk Bucket is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be used to remove [[Potions|Potion]] effects or craft certain items.
## Obtaining
### Trades
A Milk Bucket can be obtained in exchange for 50 [[Coins]] in the [[Trades]] menu after unlocking [[Leather|Leather II (2)]].
### Buckets
A Milk Bucket can be obtained by filling up a [[Bucket]] from a [[Cow]] or [[Mushroom Cow|Mooshroom]]-based [[Mob]].
## Usage
### Removing Potions
A Milk Bucket can be consumed to remove all the player's active [[Potions|Potion]] effects, including the effects of a [[God Potion]]. Doing this will turn the Milk Bucket back into a [[Bucket]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Cake]]|Raw Chicken VII (7) ([[Raw Chicken]])|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] 2x [[Enchanted Sugar]] 3x [[Wheat]] [[Milk Bucket]] 3x Milk Bucket|Recipe: Milk Bucket ×3 + [[Enchanted Sugar]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Egg]] + [[Wheat]] ×3 → [[Enchanted Cake]]|
|COMMON Beastmaster Crest ([[Beastmaster Crest]])|None|[[Milk Bucket]] 1x Milk Bucket 4x [[Griffin Feather]] 40x [[Ancient Claw]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] ×4 + [[Ancient Claw]] x10 ×4 + Milk Bucket → [[Beastmaster Crest]]|
|[[Cake]]|None|1x [[Egg]] 2x [[Sugar]] [[Milk Bucket]] 3x Milk Bucket 3x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: Milk Bucket ×3 + [[Sugar]] ×2 + [[Egg]] + [[Wheat]] ×3 → [[Cake]]|
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|You cleared all of your active effects!|Drinking a Milk Bucket|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Milk Bucket.|
Linked from: [[Accessories/Misc Crafts]], [[Beastmaster Crest]], [[Bucket]], [[Bucket (disambiguation)]], [[Cake]], [[Changelog/2021/January 26]], [[Collections]], [[Collections/List]], [[Cookie Clicker]], [[Egg]], [[Emerald Blade]], [[Enchanted Cake]], [[Enchanted Egg]], [[Enchanted Sugar]], [[Farm]], [[God Potion]], [[God Potion Effect]], [[Griffin Feather]], [[Leather]], [[Magical Milk Bucket]], [[Pest Repellent]], [[Pest Repellent MAX]], [[Sugar]], [[The Barn]], [[Trades]], [[Vanilla Objects]], [[Wheat]]

# Minecart
Categories: Vanilla objects, Disabled Features, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Minecart is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Minecart.
This item or feature is currently disabled and cannot be used.
[Minecart]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 8 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MINECART
Tooltip: COMMON
A Minecart is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|5x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×5 → Minecart|
## Usage
Minecarts currently have no usage, as they cannot be placed. Trying to place one on a [[Private Island]] will immediately drop the Minecart.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minecart with Hopper]]|None|1x [[Hopper]] [[Minecart]] 1x Minecart|Recipe: [[Hopper]] + Minecart → [[Minecart with Hopper]]|
|[[Minecart with Furnace]]|None|1x [[Furnace]] [[Minecart]] 1x Minecart|Recipe: [[Furnace]] + Minecart → [[Minecart with Furnace]]|
|[[Minecart with TNT]]|None|1x [[TNT]] [[Minecart]] 1x Minecart|Recipe: [[TNT]] + Minecart → [[Minecart with TNT]]|
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|Minecarts are limited to a maximum of 0 in SkyBlock for performance reasons.|Trying to place a Minecart on a [[Private Island]]|
|You cannot place Minecarts on the Garden.|Trying to place a Minecart on [[The Garden]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Minecart.|
|Dec 17, 2019|Disabled the ability to place Minecarts.|

## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Mushroom Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Mushroom Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Bat Pet]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Magical Mushroom Soup]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Brown Mushroom Block]] Recipe; [[Red Mushroom Block]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] Recipe; [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Night Vision Charm]] Recipe; [[Mystical Mushroom Soup]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] Recipe; [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|+50,000 [[Farming]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
### Farming
Mushrooms can be harvested at the [[Mushroom Desert]] or [[The Garden]].
### Mob Drops
Red Mushrooms drop from the following [[Mob|Mobs]]:
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv81]|[[Agarimoo]]|35|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv81]|[[Agarimoo]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|60%|
|[Lv81]|[[Agarimoo]]|15|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv167]|[[Mushroom Bull]]|4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Mushroom Cow]]|4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Minions
Red Mushrooms and Brown Mushrooms can be generated by [[Mushroom Minion|Mushroom Minions]].
### Shop Purchases
Red Mushrooms and Brown Mushrooms can be bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] for 25 [[Coins]] each.
### Treasure Hunter
16 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]] and 16 [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]] are potential rewards from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s treasure.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Magical Mushroom Soup]]|Mushroom IV (4)|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] 1x [[Redstone Dust]] 1x [[Glowstone Dust]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Glowstone Dust]] + [[Mushroom]] ×2 + [[Redstone Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Magical Mushroom Soup]]|
|Mushroom Minion I ([[Mushroom Minion]])|Mushroom I (1)|1x [[Wooden Hoe]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Hoe]] → [[Mushroom Minion]]|
|Mushroom Minion II ([[Mushroom Minion]])|Mushroom I (1)|1x Mushroom Minion I ([[Mushroom Minion]]) 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] x20 ×8 + [[Mushroom Minion]] → [[Mushroom Minion]]|
|Mushroom Minion III ([[Mushroom Minion]])|Mushroom I (1)|1x Mushroom Minion II ([[Mushroom Minion]]) 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] x40 ×8 + [[Mushroom Minion]] → [[Mushroom Minion]]|
|Mushroom Minion IV ([[Mushroom Minion]])|Mushroom I (1)|1x Mushroom Minion III ([[Mushroom Minion]]) 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] x64 ×8 + [[Mushroom Minion]] → [[Mushroom Minion]]|

# Mushroom Cow
Aliases: Mooshroom
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Mushroom Cow is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Mushroom Cow.
[Mushroom Cow]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Mushroom Desert]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 4x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Raw Beef]] 1x [[Leather]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: MUSHROOM_COW_1
Mushroom Cows are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found in [[Mushroom Gorge]], [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]], and [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]].
## Behavior
Mushroom Cows are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player. They can also be brought to [[Jake]] in the [[Desert Mountain]] using a [[Lead]] to unlock the [[Advanced Mooshroom Axe]] in his shop.

Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Raw Beef]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Leather]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|

## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Mycelium Minion I ([[Mycelium Minion]])|Mycelium I (1); 500 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|1x [[Wooden Shovel]] [[Mycelium]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Mycelium|Recipe: Mycelium x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Shovel]] → [[Mycelium Minion]]|
|Mycelium Minion II ([[Mycelium Minion]])|Mycelium I (1); 1000 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|1x Mycelium Minion I ([[Mycelium Minion]]) [[Mycelium]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Mycelium|Recipe: Mycelium x32 ×8 + [[Mycelium Minion]] → [[Mycelium Minion]]|
|Mycelium Minion III ([[Mycelium Minion]])|Mycelium I (1); 1500 ቾ Mage Reputation ([[Factions]])|1x Mycelium Minion II ([[Mycelium Minion]]) [[Mycelium]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Mycelium|Recipe: Mycelium x64 ×8 + [[Mycelium Minion]] → [[Mycelium Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Mycelium]]|Mycelium III (3)|[[Mycelium]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Mycelium|Recipe: Mycelium x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Mycelium]]|
|[[Suspicious Stew]]|Mycelium II (2)|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] 1x [[Burning Eye]] 1x [[Spectre Dust]] [[Mycelium]] 1x Mycelium|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] + [[Burning Eye]] + Mycelium + [[Spectre Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Suspicious Stew]]|
|[[Suspicious Stew]]|Mycelium II (2)|1x [[Spectre Dust]] 1x [[Bowl]] [[Mycelium]] 1x Mycelium 1x [[Burning Eye]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Mushroom]] + [[Burning Eye]] + Mycelium + [[Spectre Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Suspicious Stew]]|
## Trivia
- Not long before the leak of the information of new collections of Crimson Isle update about Mycelium and Red Sand collections, the ability of obtaining Mycelium, including spreading onto dirt and buying from trades menu, were removed. This was likely for preventing stockpiling by alpha testers.
  - As [[Crimson Isle]] released on April 20, 2022, mycelium spreading onto dirt has been re-enabled.
- Using a [[Builder's Ruler]] or [[Block Zapper]] to remove mycelium will turn it into dirt, with the following message: Your # Mycelium has turned into Dirt!
  - This happens even on [[The Garden]], where mycelium spread is disabled.
- Collecting Mycelium turned from [[Dirt]] on Private Islands does increase collection, that is unless the dirt block was placed by a player.
  - However, there are only limited ways to have Mycelium giving collection on the island: the initial island (turn [[Grass]] blocks to dirt by placing blocks right onto them), some type of placeable islands, and [[Mycelium Minion|Mycelium Minions]].
- Contrary to the low light level requirement of [[Mushroom]] spreading, Mycelium requires high light level to spread onto dirt.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Mycelium Collection.|
|Mar 8, 2022|Mycelium can now be mined in the [[Crimson Isle]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Mycelium.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Mycelium is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Feb 2, 2022|Mycelium can no longer be bought from the [[Builder]] and the [[Trades]] menu.|
|Feb 2, 2022|Disabled Mycelium spreading onto nearby dirt.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Mycelium is no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Re-enabled Mycelium spreading onto dirt.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Mycelium can be purchased in [[Bazaar]].|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Mycelium is no longer excluded from the [[Mining Sack]].|

Nether Quartz is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Collections|Collection]] item primarily obtained in the [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Abandoned Quarry]].
## Collection
Collecting Nether Quartz increases the player's Nether Quartz [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Nether Quartz.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Quartz Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Night Saver]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Day Saver]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Minion Expander]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Enchanted Quartz Block]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Night Crystal]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Day Crystal]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|[[Solar Panel]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Nether Quartz can be obtained from [[Mining]] [[Ores|Nether Quartz Ore]] in the [[Crimson Isle]], as well as [[Ores|Pure Quartz]] in the [[Abandoned Quarry]] of the [[Dwarven Mines]]. The latter of which grants much more Nether Quartz, and is easier to access.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv182]|[[Abyssal Miner]]|10|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv109]|[[Voidling]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|40%|
### Minions
Nether Quartz can be obtained from [[Quartz Minion|Quartz Minions]].

# Nether Star (disambiguation)

Nether Star (disambiguation) is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Nether Star.

## Obtaining
### Farming
Nether Wart can be harvested at the [[Farm]], the [[Crimson Isle]] or [[The Garden]].
### Minions
Nether Wart can be generated by [[Nether Wart Minion|Nether Wart Minions]].
### Shop Purchase
Nether Wart can be bought from the [[Alchemist]] for 10 [[Coins]] each or from [[Ulyn]] or [[An]] for 25 [[Coins]] each.

Netherrack is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] collection item found primarily on the [[Crimson Isle]]. It gives +0.5 [[Mining]] experience when mined.
## Collection
Collecting netherracks increases the player's netherrack [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of netherracks.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!

|III|500|[[Nether Brick]] Trade|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Netherrack]] Recipe|+4|
|V|5,000|[[Magical Lava Bucket]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Netherrack can be mined primarily on the [[Crimson Isle]].

## Usage
### Smelting Ingredient
Netherrack can be smelted in a furnace into 1 [[Nether Brick]].
### Brewing Ingredient
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Burning Potion]]|Netherrack II (2)|Netherrack +; [[Awkward Potion]]|Recipe: Netherrack → [[Wounded Potion]], [[Wounded Potion]], [[Wounded Potion]]|
### Trading
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Brick ([[Vanilla Objects]])|Netherrack III (3)|[[Netherrack]] 1 Netherrack|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to the Crimson Isle ([[Portals]])|Glowstone Dust II (2) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Glowstone]] [[Netherrack]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Netherrack|Recipe: [[Glowstone]] x16 ×3 + Netherrack x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|[[Enchanted Netherrack]]|Netherrack IV (4)|[[Netherrack]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Netherrack|Recipe: Netherrack x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Netherrack]]|
### Kuudra Follower Boots
[[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Boots]] requires 1,000,000 in Netherrack Collection and 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Netherrack]] to be given to [[Kuudra Loremaster]] to claim the boots.
## Trivia
- [[Enchantments|Smelting Touch]] [[Pickaxes]] / [[Drills]] will automatically smelt the netherrack into [[Nether Brick]]. This will not count towards the collection, so the player must use a tool without smelting touch.
- Netherrack used to be one of the hardest collections to maintain at #1, although current #1 on leaderboard 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] niqhta [2] has almost double the amount of #2.
- Netherrack is one of the only collections that does not have a [[Minions|Minion]], the others being [[Gemstone]], [[Magmafish]], [[Sulphur]] and [[Ink Sac]].
  - Since there is no [[Minions|minion]] for [[Netherrack]] Netherrack, the most efficient way to collect is to equip [[Snail Pet]] as well as wear [[Rancher's Boots]] and other [[Mineral Armor]] pieces.
- Prior to the Nether update's release, players were able to access the [[Blazing Fortress]] as early as [[Combat]] Level V. Notably, those on the [[Ironman]] gamemode could quickly reach netherrack collection V and unlock the recipe for the [[Magical Lava Bucket]].
- Netherrack can be stored in 3 different sacks ([[Nether Sack]], [[Lava Fishing Sack]] and [[Mining Sack]]) which is more than any other resource, except for [[Magma Cream]], which can also be stored in 3 sacks.

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Netherrack.|

Obsidian is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla crafting block.
## Collection
Collecting Obsidian increases the player's Obsidian [[Collections|Collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Obsidian.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Obsidian Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Lethality]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|III|250|[[Gravity Talisman]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Obsidian]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Stun Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VIII|25,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|IX|50,000|[[Obsidian Tablet]] Recipe|+4|
|X|100,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]; +50,000 [[Mining]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Naturally breakable Obsidian generates in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]], [[The End]] and [[Dwarven Mines]]. Mining Obsidian with [[Pickaxes]] / [[Drills]] with at least 4 [[Breaking Power]] grants +20 [[Mining]] XP. Obsidian can also be generated on the [[Private Island|Player's Island]] by having [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] flow over a [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] source block, or it can be made by dumping [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] into the corner of a 2x2 pool of water.

Ores are mineable blocks that spawn in certain [[Locations]]. Unlike vanilla Minecraft, ores cannot be found in most of the world. Otherwise, the ores themselves behave as expected. Many higher level ores are inaccessible via mining until reaching the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Obtaining
Ores can be found on the [[Mining Islands]] and certain areas ([[Coal Mine]], [[Gold Mine]], [[Deep Caverns]], [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] and [[Crimson Isle]]).
- The amount of items received when mined manually is influenced by [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Ore Fortune]].
- Their type determine the minimum [[Breaking Power]] the player must have to mine them.
- Their location determine their generation, the blocks they are replaced with when mined and their regeneration property.
  Location: [[Coal Mine]], Generation: Random, Replacement: [[Stone]], Regeneration: ✔; Location: [[Gold Mine]], Generation: Random, Replacement: [[Stone]], Regeneration: ✔; Location: [[Deep Caverns]], Generation: Random, Replacement: [[Stone]], Regeneration: ✔; Location: [[Dwarven Mines]], Generation: Not random, Replacement: [[Bedrock]], Regeneration: ✔; Location: [[Crystal Hollows]], Generation: Not random, Replacement: Air, Regeneration: ✔ in structure (i.e. [[Mines of Divan]])✕ Elsewhere; Location: [[Glacite Tunnels]], Generation: ✕; Location: [[Glacite Mineshafts]], Generation: [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.), Replacement: Air, Regeneration: ✕; Location: [[Crimson Isle]], Generation: Not random for Nether Quartz Ore ([[Ores]]) Random for Sulphur Ore ([[Sulphur]]), Replacement: [[Bedrock]] for Nether Quartz Ore ([[Ores]])Air for Sulphur Ore ([[Sulphur]]), Regeneration: ✔ for Nether Quartz Ore ([[Ores]])✕ for Sulphur Ore ([[Sulphur]])
### Oddities
- Unlike vanilla Minecraft, mining [[Ores|Iron]] and [[Ores|Gold Ores]] will not drop their Raw variant. Depending on the following, the player will either obtain [[Iron Ingot]] [[Gold Ingot]] Ingots or Ore Blocks.
  [[Private Island]] or [[Gold Mine]] or [[Deep Caverns]], without the [[Smelting Touch]] enchantment; Anywhere else, using the [[Silk Touch]] enchantment; Ingots:: [[Private Island]] or [[Gold Mine]] or [[Deep Caverns]], using the [[Smelting Touch]] enchantment; Anywhere else, without using the [[Silk Touch]] enchantment; Similarly, Iron ([[Iron Minion]]) and Gold Minions ([[Gold Minion]]) equipped with an [[Auto Smelter]] will respectively produce Iron ([[Iron Ingot]]) or Gold Ingots ([[Gold Ingot]]) instead of the Ore Blocks.
- [[Sulphur|Sulphur Ores]] only spawn during [[Blazing Volcano|Volcanic Eruptions]], shot out of the tip of the [[Blazing Volcano]], turning into solid blocks upon impact.
- The [[Goblin Pet]] grants 1250 [[Mining Speed]] on Regular Ores while [[Block Strength|not on the ground]]. This is a hidden buff.
- The Strong Arm perk from [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM Tier 8]] grants 5-500 [[Mining Speed]] on Pure Ores. This is a hidden buff.
## List of Ores
While unofficial, the community has made a distinction on Ores.
- If it bears the "ore block" form when found, then it is designated as a Regular Ore;
- If it bears the texture of the block that is crafted from it, then it is called a Pure Ore. These drop considerably more loot than their Regular counterparts, though they will also take longer to mine because of their higher [[Block Strength]].
|Appearance|Ore|Result|Location|Can spawn Golden ([[Golden Goblin]]) or Diamond Goblins ([[Diamond Goblin]])|

### [[Fetchur]]
Prior to the Minor Patch of 26 Sep 2023, [[Fetchur]] sometimes requested 50 [[Ores|Nether Quartz Ore]].
## Trivia
- Despite their name, Mithril Ore and Titanium Ore are [[Dwarven Metals]], not Ores.
## Bugs
- [[Goblin Pet]] grants 1250 [[Mining Speed]] on Ores[More Info Needed] while [[Block Strength|not on the ground]].

# Parched
Categories: Vanilla objects, Critters, Confirmations needed, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs
Parched is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Parched.
[Parched]
Type: Critter ([[Critters]])
Mob Level: 58
Mob Type: [[Woodland]] Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 25,000; [[Damage]]: 250
[Drops]
Drops: 4–8x [[Enchanted Helix Log]] 2–4x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]]
XP: +87 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 63
[Metadata]
ID: PARCHED_58
A Parched is a [[Mob]] that can be spawned by applying Honey to [[Helix Log|Helix Trees]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
A Parched has a 33.3% (1 in 3) chance to spawn after applying a [[Fun-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Fun-Sized]], [[Family-Sized Pot of Honeycomb|Family-Sized]], [[Jumbo Pot of Honeycomb|Jumbo]], or [[Behemoth Pot of Honeycomb]] to a [[Helix Log|Helix Tree]] with a [[Honey|Tree Protection Order]].
## Behavior
A Parched will slowly move around like a [[Zombie]]. Being a [[Woodland]] mob, it can only be attacked with an [[Axes|Axe]] or [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]]. It will attack the player when they attack them. Parched can also heal itself once[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] for 25% of its max [[Health]].
## Drops

|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Honeycomb]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Enchanted Helix Log]]|4-8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv58]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|19|19|19|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|87|87|87|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|63|63|63|
Parched can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Parched Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Pickaxes
Aliases: Pickaxe, Wooden pickaxe
Categories: Vanilla objects, Tools, Mining
[Pickaxes]
Pickaxes are [[Mining]] tools used to obtain [[Ores|Ore]] and other rock-type blocks. How Pickaxes behave can differ based on what [[Enchantments]] and [[Reforging|Reforges]] are applied to it. Each Pickaxe also has its own [[Breaking Power]], which affects the blocks it can mine and the damage it deals to [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs.
Outside of areas with the custom Mining system implemented, the pickaxes will only be able to receive drops from blocks as their vanilla form does. For example, [[Stonk]] cannot receive drops from mining Iron Ore or Obsidian.

## Vanilla Pickaxes
Pickaxes is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Pickaxes.
All vanilla Minecraft tools exist in SkyBlock. Since tools don't use durability in SkyBlock, before the 0.11 Dwarven Mines Update golden pickaxes were actually used often as they had the biggest [[Mining Speed]] compared to all the other pickaxes. They also had a [[Breaking Power]] of 2 and now have a [[Breaking Power]] of 1. The wooden pickaxe is used in various Mining [[Minions|Minion]] recipes, but changing the type of pickaxe will not work in a minion.

|[[Stone Pickaxe]]|Stone|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|2|110|20|2 [[Coins]]|
|[[Golden Pickaxe]]|Golden|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|250|15|6 [[Coins]]|
|[[Iron Pickaxe]]|Iron|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|3|160|25|4 [[Coins]]|
|[[Diamond Pickaxe]]|Diamond|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Diamond]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|4|220|30|12 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Pioneer Pickaxe]] is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] pickaxe given to players who tested the [[Crystal Hollows]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]]. It only has a [[Breaking Power]] of 1 and a [[Mining Speed]] of +1.

# Pig
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Pig is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Pig.
[Pig]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[The Barn]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Raw Porkchop]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: FARMER_PIG_1
Pigs are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found in [[The Barn]].
## Behavior
Pigs are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player. They can also be brought to [[Jake]] in the [[Desert Mountain]] using a [[Lead]] to unlock the [[Advanced Pig Axe]] in his shop.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Raw Porkchop]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|The Farming Islands ([[Bestiary/List]])|Pig|Smells like Bacon.|1|5|200|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|40|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|60|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|100|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|200|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Pig.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the [[Coins]] from 0 to 1 and [[Combat]] XP from 3 to 5.|

# Pig (Private Island)
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Pig (Private Island) is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Pig.
[Pig]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50-150
[Drops]
Drops: 1–2x [[Raw Porkchop]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: PIG_#
Pigs are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found on the [[Private Island]] and from [[Pig Minion|Pig Minions]].
## Spawning
Pigs spawn naturally on [[Grass]] on the [[Private Island]], and from [[Pig Minion|Pig Minions]].
### Stranded
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], Pigs that spawn in [[Biome Stick|Forest Biomes]] will have triple health and double drops.
## Behavior
Pigs are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.

# Piston
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items
Piston is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Piston.
[Piston]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 8 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PISTON_BASE
Tooltip: COMMON
A Piston is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be crafted and placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Redstone Dust]] 1x [[Iron Ingot]] 3x [[Planks]] 4x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Planks]] ×3 + [[Cobblestone]] ×4 + [[Iron Ingot]] + [[Redstone Dust]] → Piston|
## Usage
### Private Island
Pistons can be placed as a decorative block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. However, they cannot be activated from [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]].
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|Please note that pistons are currently disabled on this island!|Placing a Piston|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Piston.|

# Planks
Aliases: Acacia Planks, Acacia Wood Plank, Acacia Wood Planks, Birch Planks, Birch Wood Plank, Birch Wood Planks, Dark Oak Planks, Dark Oak Wood Plank, Dark Oak Wood Planks, Jungle Planks, Jungle Wood Plank, Jungle Wood Planks, Oak Planks, Oak Wood Plank, Oak Wood Planks, Spruce Planks, Spruce Wood Plank, Spruce Wood Planks
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Blocks, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Planks is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Planks.
[Planks]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Tool ([[Tools]]): Axe ([[Axes]])
[Block Drops]
Normal Drop: Plank
[[Silk Touch]] Drop: Plank
[[Smelting Touch]] Drop: Plank
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 0.5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WOOD (Oak Planks); WOOD:1 (Spruce Planks); WOOD:2 (Birch Planks); WOOD:3 (Jungle Planks); WOOD:4 (Acacia Planks); WOOD:5 (Dark Oak Planks)
Tooltip: COMMON
Planks can be crafted by using a log of a specific type to get a corresponding plank. These planks can be used to build and for crafting [[Stick|Sticks]] and wooden [[Tools]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Oak Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
|Birch Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Birch Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
|Spruce Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Spruce Log]]|Recipe: [[Spruce Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
|Dark Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Dark Oak Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
|Acacia Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Acacia Log]]|Recipe: [[Acacia Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
|Jungle Planks ([[Planks]])|None|1x [[Jungle Log]]|Recipe: [[Jungle Log]] → [[Planks]] x4|
### [[Builder]]
|Cost|Item|
|-|-|
|2 [[Coins]]|Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|
|2 [[Coins]]|Birch Planks ([[Planks]])|
|2 [[Coins]]|Spruce Planks ([[Planks]])|
|2 [[Coins]]|Dark Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|
|2 [[Coins]]|Acacia Planks ([[Planks]])|
|2 [[Coins]]|Jungle Planks ([[Planks]])|
### Efficient Axe
Breaking Wood Log blocks with [[Efficient Axe]], grants the player 5 of the Wood's respective Planks type. This is not affected by [[Foraging Fortune]] nor [[Sweep]].
## Usage
### Crafting

## Trivia
- The name of Planks in Hypixel SkyBlock is Plank, while the original name of the planks in vanilla Minecraft is Planks. (e.g. Oak Wood Plank in SkyBlock, not Oak Wood Planks)
