# Player
Aliases: Inventory
Categories: Vanilla objects
Player is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Player.
[Player]
[[Health]]: 100
[[Defense]]: 0
[[Strength]]: 0
[[Speed]]: 100
[[Crit Chance]]: 30%
[[Crit Damage]]: 50%
[[Intelligence]]: 0
[[Mana]]: 100
The player is the representation of the real person. All actions in real life control the player.
## Stats
The player has several base [[Stats]] that affect their gameplay. These are categorised into Combat Stats, Mining Stats, Farming Stats, Foraging Stats, Fishing Stats, Miscellaneous Stats, Hunting Stats, and Wisdom Stats.
### Effective Health
[[Health]] represents how much health the player has: when the player's health reaches 0, the player [[Death|dies]]. Due to the lack of a designated hunger system in [[SkyBlock]], saturation does not determine the rate at which [[Health]] regenerates: instead, the player's [[Health Regen]] determines the player's healing rate, and the player's [[Vitality]] allows for healing abilities to be used. The player's [[Mending]] affects all outgoing healing, which increases how much the player heals other players.
[[Defense]] represents the player's damage reduction: the more [[Defense]] the player has, the less damage they take.
The combined effects of [[Health]] and [[Defense]], while not visualized as a stat in-game, can be represented with [[Effective Health]].
### Damage
The player's total [[Damage]] is dependent on their [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], and the [[Damage]] of their equipped weapon. Additionally, external modifiers can increase the player's damage output, such as the [[Warden Helmet]] or [[Golden Dragon Pet]].
The player's [[Strength]] serves as a [[Damage]] multiplier, increasing the damage output of the player. Likewise, the player's [[Crit Damage]] serves as a [[Damage]] multiplier towards critical hits, which is dependent on the player's [[Crit Chance]].
In most cases, it is better to balance the player's [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] as evenly as possible: the closer the two stats are, the higher the player's [[Damage Calculation|Final Damage]] will be.
### Swing Range
[[Swing Range]] determines the range at which the player can hit enemies with melee weapons. Similar to vanilla Minecraft, the player's base [[Swing Range]] is 3 : this can be increased with [[Longswords]] or items such as the [[Giraffe Pet]].
It is important to note that [[Swing Range]] does not affect the player's interaction range: higher [[Swing Range]] will not allow the player to mine or break blocks from further, or the like.
### Attack Speed
[[Attack Speed]] determines two things: the reduction of invulnerability ticks an enemy is granted upon hitting them, and the fire rate of [[Shortbows]]. The maximum [[Attack Speed]] is 100 [[Attack Speed]], which doubles the rate of attack. [[Thermodynamic Armor]] increases the cap by +50% .
## Movement
### Walking and Sprinting
Walking and sprinting are the most basic form of movement in [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]], and is traditionally bound to W and ⌃ Ctrl respectively.
The player's [[Speed]] determines the rate at which the player walks: the base [[Speed]] cap is 400 , which can be increased by items such as the [[Racing Helmet]] and [[Unalloyed Speed]] pet item. Additionally, some items will reduce the player's speed, such as the [[Warden Helmet]] or [[Heavy Armor]].
Due to the lack of hunger in [[SkyBlock]], players are able to sprint indefinitely without loss of hunger or a similar stamina system.
By holding ⇧ Shift, the player can sneak where they move slower and cannot fall off the edges of blocks. Players can still move off of a block by jumping off of them, or by walking onto a slab or stair.
### Jumping
Jumping allows the player to leap into the air and is traditionally bound to Space. By default, the player is able to jump 1.25 blocks high: this can be increased with the [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]] effect.
Several items can be equipped or consumed to increase the player's [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]], such as the [[Jump Boost Potion]], [[Horse Pet]] and [[Balloon Snake]]: however, the most significant source of [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]] is the [[God Potion]], which grants [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost IV]].
### Swimming
Swimming allows the player to swim through water without being slowed while the player is fully submerged, and is traditionally bound to the same keybinds as sprinting. The rate at which a player swims and moves in water can be increased with [[Depth Strider]].
## Experience
[[Experience]] is a system that increments with obtaining Experience Orbs. It can be obtained from multiple sources, including but not limited to killing mobs, fishing, and mining [[Ores]] and [[Dwarven Metals]]: however, it is primarily obtained from [[Experience Bottle|Experience Bottles]] and its higher tiers.
[[Experience]] is used for [[Enchantments|enchanting]] items at an [[Enchantment Table]], combining enchantments in an [[Anvil]], and accessing the [[Experimentation Table]]. Enchanting items does not cost [[Lapis Lazuli]], and combining items in an [[Anvil]] will not increase the item's upgrade or repair cost.
## Interface
The player's interface, also known as the HUD (heads-up display), showcases vital information for the player. It consists of the health bar, hunger bar, experience bar, hotbar, scoreboard, and boss bar.
### Hotbar
The hotbar is a selection bar that appears at the bottom of the screen, where the player's selected items can be quickly accessed: the player can swap through their hotbar through 1-9 or through scrolling.
The [[SkyBlock Menu]] is permanently bound to the ninth hotbar slot. While there are arrows in the [[Quiver]] or [[Bait|Fishing Baits]] in the [[Bait Sack]], holding a [[Bows|Bow]] changes the menu from a Nether Star to an Arrow; holding a [[Shortbows|Shortbow]] changes the menu to a Feather; holding a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] changes the menu to the player's selected [[Bait|Fishing Bait]]; and holding any [[Scaffolding]] changes the menu to show how much Scaffolding the player can place.
The offhand, normally accessible through F, is notably disabled in SkyBlock.
### Action bar
The action bar displays the player's [[Health]], [[Defense]] [[Mana]], and [[Vitality]]. If the player has 0 [[Defense]], it is omitted from the action bar: additionally, if the player has [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] active, it is displayed beside the player's [[Mana]].
When the player uses an ability or enters a new [[Locations|location]], the [[Defense]] marker is replaced with its respective text. For example:
- -# Mana (<ability name>)
- <zone name>
The vanilla stat bars consist of the health, hunger, and armor bars. While the health bar adapts to the player's [[Health]] and [[Absorption]], the lack of a hunger system and most sets of armor being a variation of dyed Leather Armor results in the hunger bar and armor bar being largely obsolete.
### Scoreboard
The scoreboard displays the player's in-real-life date, alongside the [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]], current [[Locations|Location]], number of [[Coins]] in the [[Purse]] and [[Bits]] available (if applicable), among other useful information, including but not limited to:
- most currencies (e.g. [[Copper]], ✿ [[Sowdust]], [[Essence]])
- the duration of certain events (e.g. [[Carnival]], [[Spooky Festival]], [[Traveling Zoo]])
- displaying active [[Quests]]
### Boss Bar
The boss bar is used in a multitude of ways:
- displaying active [[Quests]]
- displaying the health of boss mobs: most notably [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], [[Catacombs]] bosses, and [[Kuudra]]
- serving as an alternative to the player locator bar in the [[Catacombs]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Players can now enter [[SkyBlock]].|

# Poison
Categories: Vanilla objects, Potions, Brewing
Poison is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Poison.
Poison is a potion effect that continuously drains [[Health]]. Poison deals [[True Damage]], meaning it isn't affected by [[Defense]]. This effect cannot kill you; it will stop at the vanilla equivalent of a half heart. The Poison effect can be obtained by:
- Splashing yourself with or drinking a [[Poison Potion]]
- Getting splashed with a Poison Potion from a [[Witch]]
- Getting hit by a [[Cave Spider]], either spawned by the [[Broodmother]] or a [[Cave Spider Minion]]
The [[Vaccine Talisman]] and the [[Cow Hat]] provide immunity from the poison effect. Not to be confused with the debuff inflicted by [[Arachne]] and [[Arachne's Keeper]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Poison.|
Linked from: [[Blindness Potion]], [[Damage Calculation]]

Potato is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Potato.
[Potato]

## Obtaining
### Farming
Potatoes can be harvested at [[The Barn]] or [[The Garden]].
### Minions
Potatoes can be generated by [[Potato Minion|Potato Minions]], or by [[Minions]] with a [[Potato Spreading]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]].
### Mob Drops

|[Lv2]|[[Zombie Villager]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv2-28]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv2]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Shop Purchases
Potatoes can be bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] for 10 [[Coins]] each.
### Treasure Hunter
[[Potato]] 16 Potato is a potential reward from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s treasure.

|Potato Minion II ([[Potato Minion]])|Potato I (1)|1x Potato Minion I ([[Potato Minion]]) [[Potato]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Potato|Recipe: Potato x32 ×8 + [[Potato Minion]] → [[Potato Minion]]|
|Potato Minion III ([[Potato Minion]])|Potato I (1)|1x Potato Minion II ([[Potato Minion]]) [[Potato]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Potato|Recipe: Potato x64 ×8 + [[Potato Minion]] → [[Potato Minion]]|
## Gallery
- Potato in the Greenhouse Potato in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Trivia
- From November 2019 to June 2020, The Great Potato War was a battle between Technoblade and im_a_squid_kid to hold the number one position for the Potato leaderboard. There were guilds dedicated to helping both sides of the war.
  - Technoblade needed large amounts of [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] for his gear. Instead of buying them from the [[Auction House]], he opted to use his own [[Potato Minion|Potato Minions]] instead. After acquiring all the [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] he needed, he discovered he was in a position to compete for the top ranking on the Potato leaderboard. He embarked on his mission to attain and hold the number one leaderboard position for Potatoes.
  - With both sides weary from continued battle, Technoblade put forth a compromise to decide the winner once and for all. Whomever reached 500 million potato collection first would be declared the winner. This was later commented on by Hypixel saying that the winner would receive a reward.
  - The war was finished on 21st of June, 2020 with Technoblade winning the war with exactly 500 million potatoes. Squid only had 412 million potatoes by this time. To celebrate his victory, players were able to claim the [[Potato Talisman]] from [[Potato King]] for a period after his victory.
- When [[Potato Spreading]] was first released, an item called "Potatoes" was generated in minions, rather than normal Potatoes.
- Relevant Videos to the Potato War:
  - Skyblock: The Great Potato War
  - Skyblock: Potato War 2
  - Skyblock: Potato War 3 (FINALE)
  - The Whitelisted Mystery

|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased the NPC sell price of Potato from 1 Coin ([[Coins]]) to 4 [[Coins]].|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Decreased the NPC sell price of Potato from 4 [[Coins]] to 3 [[Coins]].|

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Prismarine can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 10 [[Coins]].
### Pond Island
The [[Pond Island]] can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]], which comes with 368 Prismarine in the structure.

|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|

### Private Island
Prismarine can be placed as a decorative block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
### Mithril Ore
Breakable Prismarine on [[Mining Islands]] represents [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]], which has a [[Block Strength]] of 800 and can be broken to obtain 2 [[Mithril]] and 3 [[Mithril Powder]].

## Obtaining
### Fishing
Prismarine Shard can be fished from any water source block available. It is a common item in the [[Fishing|Fishing Collection]].
### Minions
Prismarine Shard can be obtained using the [[Fishing Minion]]. This method is best for low level fishing or for overnight fishing experience.
### Sea Creatures
Prismarine Shard can be obtained by killing [[Sea Guardian|Sea Guardians]].

### Quiver
Prismarine Shard can be placed in the [[Quiver]] to be used as projectiles for the [[Prismarine Bow]].
## Trivia
- Since Prismarine shards count as Ammunition, they go straight into the Quiver when fished and don't count towards the [[Collections|Collection]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
  - A work-around for that is to fill the quiver with other items (f.e. arrows), so that newly fished Prismarine Shards go into the inventory instead.

## Collection

## Obtaining
### Farming
Pumpkins can be harvested in the [[The Barn]] or [[The Garden]].
### Minions
Pumpkins can be generated by [[Pumpkin Minion|Pumpkin Minions]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv69]|[[Werewolf]]|16-32|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv77]|[[Nightmare]]|8-16|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv32]|[[Scarecrow]]|4-8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv28]|[[Jumpin' Jack]]|2-4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv102]|[[Crazy Witch (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv92]|[[Phantom Spirit (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv95]|[[Scary Jerry (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv92]|[[Trick or Treater (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv94]|[[Wither Gourd (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv99]|[[Wraith (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Shop Purchases
Pumpkins can be bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] for 25 [[Coins]] each.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|Pumpkin III (3)|[[Pumpkin]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|
|[[Farmer Orb]]|Pumpkin II (2)|[[Pumpkin]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x12 ×8 → [[Farmer Orb]]|
|[[Spooky Bait]]|Pumpkin IV (4)|[[Pumpkin]] 1x Pumpkin 1x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: Pumpkin + [[Raw Cod]] → [[Spooky Bait]]|
|Pumpkin Minion I ([[Pumpkin Minion]])|Pumpkin I (1)|1x [[Wooden Hoe]] [[Pumpkin]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Hoe]] → [[Pumpkin Minion]]|
|Pumpkin Minion II ([[Pumpkin Minion]])|Pumpkin I (1)|1x Pumpkin Minion I ([[Pumpkin Minion]]) [[Pumpkin]] 160x Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x20 ×8 + [[Pumpkin Minion]] → [[Pumpkin Minion]]|
|Pumpkin Minion III ([[Pumpkin Minion]])|Pumpkin I (1)|1x Pumpkin Minion II ([[Pumpkin Minion]]) [[Pumpkin]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x40 ×8 + [[Pumpkin Minion]] → [[Pumpkin Minion]]|
|Pumpkin Minion IV ([[Pumpkin Minion]])|Pumpkin I (1)|1x Pumpkin Minion III ([[Pumpkin Minion]]) [[Pumpkin]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin x64 ×8 + [[Pumpkin Minion]] → [[Pumpkin Minion]]|
|[[Pumpkin Pie]]|None|[[Pumpkin]] 1x Pumpkin 1x [[Sugar]] 1x [[Egg]]|Recipe: Pumpkin + [[Sugar]] + [[Egg]] → [[Pumpkin Pie]]|
|[[Pumpkin Seeds]]|None|[[Pumpkin]] 1x Pumpkin|Recipe: Pumpkin → [[Pumpkin Seeds]] x4|
|[[Jack o' Lantern]]|None|[[Pumpkin]] 1x Pumpkin 1x [[Torch]]|Recipe: Pumpkin + [[Torch]] → [[Jack o' Lantern]]|
## Gallery
- Pumpkin in the Greenhouse Pumpkin in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Trivia
- Pumpkins can be worn on the player's Helmet slot to prevent [[Enderman|Endermen]] from becoming aggressive towards the player when looked at, though they can greatly obstruct the player's view.
- Huge pumpkin farms are currently the second best way to grind for [[Farming|Farming XP]] due to them giving +4.5 [[Farming|Farming XP]] compared to most other crops’ +4 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## History

|Dec 3, 2020|Added Pumpkin XI (11).|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased the NPC sell price of Pumpkin from 4 [[Coins]] to 10 [[Coins]].|

# Rabbit
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
Rabbit is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Rabbit.
[Rabbit]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[Oasis]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 130
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Raw Rabbit]] 0–1x [[Rabbit's Foot]] 0–1x [[Rabbit Hide]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: FARMING_RABBIT_1
Rabbits are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found in the [[Oasis]] section of the [[Mushroom Desert]].
## Behavior
Rabbits are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player. They can also be brought to [[Jake]] in the [[Desert Mountain]] using a [[Lead]] to unlock the [[Advanced Rabbit Axe]] in his shop.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Raw Rabbit]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Rabbit Hide]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|70%|
|[[Rabbit's Foot]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|70%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|The Farming Islands ([[Bestiary/List]])|Rabbit|Never stops hopping.|1|5|200|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Strength]]|[[Magic Find]]|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20|+2 [[Strength]]|+2 [[Magic Find]]|+2%|+0.5|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|40|+4 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Magic Find]]|+4%|+1|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|60|+6 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Magic Find]]|+6%|+1.5|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|100|+8 [[Strength]]|+8 [[Magic Find]]|+8%|+2|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|200|+10 [[Strength]]|+10 [[Magic Find]]|+10%|+2.5|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

## Trivia
- Rabbits used to be able to be spawned from [[Fishing]] using the [[Farmer's Rod]].

# Rabbit (Private Island)

Rabbits are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found on the [[Private Island]] and from [[Rabbit Minion|Rabbit Minions]].
## Spawning
Rabbits spawn naturally on [[Grass]] on the [[Private Island]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; can they spawn on normal profile; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], and from [[Rabbit Minion|Rabbit Minions]].
### Stranded
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
Rabbits spawn in [[Biome Stick|Taiga Biomes]], [[Biome Stick|Desert Biomes]] and [[Biome Stick|Roofed Forest Biomes]] during the day.
## Behavior
Rabbits are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.

[Item Metadata]

Rabbit Hide is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla item used in the [[Raw Rabbit]] collection, primarily to make [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]]. They are dropped by [[Rabbit|Rabbits]], and can be obtained from a [[Rabbit Minion]].
## Obtaining

|[Lv1]|[[Rabbit (Private Island)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|70%|

Rabbit Hide can be obtained from [[Rabbit Minion|Rabbit Minions]].

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Leather]]|None|[[Rabbit Hide]] 4x Rabbit Hide|Recipe: Rabbit Hide ×4 → [[Leather]]|
|[[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]]|Raw Rabbit VI (6) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|[[Rabbit Hide]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Rabbit Hide|Recipe: Rabbit Hide x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]]|

|Rabbit Chestplate ([[Rabbit Armor]])|Raw Rabbit II (2) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|[[Rabbit Hide]] 8x Rabbit Hide|Recipe: Rabbit Hide ×8 → [[Rabbit Armor]]|
|Rabbit Leggings ([[Rabbit Armor]])|Raw Rabbit II (2) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|[[Rabbit Hide]] 7x Rabbit Hide|Recipe: Rabbit Hide ×7 → [[Rabbit Armor]]|
|Rabbit Boots ([[Rabbit Armor]])|Raw Rabbit II (2) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|[[Rabbit Hide]] 4x Rabbit Hide|Recipe: Rabbit Hide ×4 → [[Rabbit Armor]]|

### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|

### Private Island
Rails can be placed as a decorative block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. However, [[Minecart|Minecarts]] cannot be placed on them.

|[[Enchanted Raw Beef]]|Leather V (5) ([[Leather]])|[[Raw Beef]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Raw Beef|Recipe: Raw Beef x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Raw Beef]]|
